The natural gas found in only five cities before 2002 reached 81 cities and 541 residential units as of today. Our natural gas use population has reached 66.5 million and is 81 per cent of our total population. In the first 11 months of 2019, 141 oil and gas exploration, production and detection wells were opened and drilled 313,000 metres.
